TUR_minority_revolt_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
					resistance > 74.9
				}
			}
			every_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
					resistance > 74.9
				}
				controller = { country_event = { id = ottoman.0 hours = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

TUR_reduce_armenian_christian_level = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { TUR_armenian_state_level < 2 } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_enemy_local_supplies_armenian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_enemy_local_supplies_armenian
			min = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_attrition_armenian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_attrition_armenian
			min = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_army_defence_factor_armenian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_army_defence_factor_armenian
			max = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_armenian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_armenian
			max = 0
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { state = 352 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.101 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 806 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.103 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 1022 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.134 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 881 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.135 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 355 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.136 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 1025 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.135 days = 4 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_resistance = yes
				is_core_of = ROOT
				TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
			}
			cancel_resistance = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = ARM }
			remove_core_of = ARM
			if = {
				limit = { state = 353 }
				354 = { remove_claim_by = ARM }
				1034 = { remove_claim_by = ARM }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_reduce_armenian_population
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_armenian_minority
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_attrition_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_attrition_armenian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_attrition_armenian
			min = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_army_defence_factor_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_army_defence_factor_armenian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_army_defence_factor_armenian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_enemy_local_supplies_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_enemy_local_supplies_armenian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_enemy_local_supplies_armenian
			min = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_recruitable_population_armenian_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_armenian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_armenian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
	}
	add_to_variable = { TUR_armenian_state_level = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_level = -20 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_apostolic_state_level = -1 }
}
TUR_reduce_armenian_christian_level_genocide = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { TUR_armenian_state_level < 2 } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
		set_variable = { TUR_ottoman_weekly_manpower_armenian = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_enemy_local_supplies_armenian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_enemy_local_supplies_armenian
			min = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_attrition_armenian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_attrition_armenian
			min = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_army_defence_factor_armenian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_army_defence_factor_armenian
			max = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_armenian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_armenian
			max = 0
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { state = 352 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.101 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 806 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.103 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 1022 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.134 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 881 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.135 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 355 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.136 days = 4 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { state = 1025 }
				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.135 days = 4 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_resistance = yes
				is_core_of = ROOT
				TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
			}
			cancel_resistance = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = ARM }
			remove_core_of = ARM
			if = {
				limit = { state = 353 }
				354 = { remove_claim_by = ARM }
				1034 = { remove_claim_by = ARM }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_reduce_armenian_population
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_armenian_minority
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_mehmed_resid_committee } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = monthly_population_100
		}
		else = { custom_effect_tooltip = weekly_manpower_250 }
		set_variable = { TUR_ottoman_weekly_manpower_armenian = 0 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_attrition_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_attrition_armenian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_attrition_armenian
			min = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_army_defence_factor_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_army_defence_factor_armenian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_army_defence_factor_armenian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_enemy_local_supplies_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_enemy_local_supplies_armenian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_enemy_local_supplies_armenian
			min = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_recruitable_population_armenian_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_armenian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_armenian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
	}
	add_to_variable = { TUR_armenian_state_level = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_level = -20 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_apostolic_state_level = -1 }
}

		limit = { check_variable = { TUR_greek_state_level < 2 } }

		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_local_intel_to_enemies_greek
			min = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_greek = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_greek

		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_state_production_speed_buildings_factor_greek
			min = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_resistance = yes
				is_core_of = ROOT
				TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
			}
			cancel_resistance = yes

		if = {

			remove_core_of = GRE

		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = PON }
			remove_core_of = PON
			if = {
				limit = { state = 354 }
				1034 = { remove_claim_by = PON }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {

		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_local_intel_to_enemies_greek
			min = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_recruitable_population_greek_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_greek = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_greek
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_local_construction_speed_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_state_production_speed_buildings_factor_greek = -0.02 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_greek
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
	}
	add_to_variable = { TUR_greek_state_level = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_level = -20 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_orthodox_state_level = -1 }
}

TUR_reduce_greek_muslim_level = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { TUR_greek_sunni_state_level > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { TUR_greek_state_level < 2 } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_local_resources_factor_greek = 0.05 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = TUR_ottoman_local_resources_factor_greek
				max = 0
			}
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_local_intel_to_enemies_greek = -0.1 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = TUR_ottoman_local_intel_to_enemies_greek
				min = 0
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = TUR_greek_sunni_state_level
					NOT = { check_variable = { TUR_greek_sunni_state_level = 0 } }
				}
				add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_target_greek = 0.05 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_state_production_speed_buildings_factor_greek = -0.02 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = TUR_ottoman_state_production_speed_buildings_factor_greek
				min = 0
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_resistance = yes
					is_core_of = ROOT
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
				}
				cancel_resistance = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_reduce_greek_population
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_greek_minority
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_local_resources_factor_greek_down
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_local_resources_factor_greek = 0.05 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = TUR_ottoman_local_resources_factor_greek
				max = 0
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_local_intel_to_enemies_down
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_local_intel_to_enemies_greek = -0.1 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = TUR_ottoman_local_intel_to_enemies_greek
				min = 0
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = TUR_greek_sunni_state_level
					NOT = { check_variable = { TUR_greek_sunni_state_level = 0 } }
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_resistance_target_down
				add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_target_greek = 0.05 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_local_construction_speed_down
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_state_production_speed_buildings_factor_greek = -0.02 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_greek
				max = 0
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_greek_state_level = -1 }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_greek_sunni_state_level = -20 }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sunni_state_level = -1 }
	}
}

TUR_reduce_arab_muslim_level = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { TUR_arab_state_level < 2 } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_local_supplies_for_controller_arab = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_local_supplies_for_controller_arab
			max = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = TUR_arab_sunni_state_level
				NOT = { check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_level = 0 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_target_arab = 0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_resistance = yes
				is_core_of = ROOT
				TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
			}
			cancel_resistance = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_reduce_arab_population
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_arab_minority
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = TUR_arab_sunni_state_level
				NOT = { check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_level = 0 } }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_resistance_target_down
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_target_arab = 0.05 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_local_supplies_for_controller_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_local_supplies_for_controller_arab = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_local_supplies_for_controller_arab
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
	}
	add_to_variable = { TUR_arab_state_level = -1 }
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = TUR_arab_sunni_state_level
				NOT = { check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_level = 0 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_level = -20 }
			add_to_variable = { TUR_sunni_state_level = -1 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { TUR_arab_shia_state_level = -20 }
			add_to_variable = { TUR_shia_state_level = -1 }
		}
	}
}
TUR_reduce_arab_christian_level = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { TUR_arab_state_level < 2 } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_local_supplies_for_controller_arab = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_local_supplies_for_controller_arab
			max = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_resistance = yes
				is_core_of = ROOT
				TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
			}
			cancel_resistance = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_reduce_arab_population
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_arab_minority
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_local_supplies_for_controller_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_local_supplies_for_controller_arab = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_local_supplies_for_controller_arab
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
	}
	add_to_variable = { TUR_arab_state_level = -1 }
	hidden_effect = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = TUR_arab_orthodox_state_level }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_orthodox_state_level = 0 }
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { TUR_arab_orthodox_state_level = -20 }
				add_to_variable = { TUR_orthodox_state_level = -1 }
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = TUR_arab_catholic_state_level }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_catholic_state_level = 0 }
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { TUR_arab_catholic_state_level = -20 }
				add_to_variable = { TUR_catholic_state_level = -1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

TUR_reduce_bulgarian_christian_level = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_state_level < 2 } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_decay_bulgarian = 0.1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_resistance_decay_bulgarian
			max = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_required_garrison_factor_bulgarian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_required_garrison_factor_bulgarian
			min = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_bulgarian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_bulgarian
			max = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_resistance = yes
				is_core_of = ROOT
				TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
			}
			cancel_resistance = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = BUL }
			remove_core_of = BUL
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = MAC }
			remove_core_of = MAC
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = SER }
			remove_core_of = SER
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = MNT }
			remove_core_of = MNT
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = BOS }
			remove_core_of = BOS
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = CRO }
			remove_core_of = CRO
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = SLV }
			remove_core_of = SLV
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_reduce_bulgarian_population
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_bulgarian_minority
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_resistance_decay_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_decay_bulgarian = 0.1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_resistance_decay_bulgarian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_required_garrison_factor_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_required_garrison_factor_bulgarian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_required_garrison_factor_bulgarian
			min = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_recruitable_population_bulgarian_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_bulgarian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_bulgarian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
	}
	add_to_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_state_level = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_state_level = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_level = -20 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_orthodox_state_level = -1 }
}
TUR_reduce_bulgarian_christian_level_genocide = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_state_level < 2 } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
		set_variable = { TUR_ottoman_weekly_manpower_bulgarian = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_decay_bulgarian = 0.1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_resistance_decay_bulgarian
			max = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_required_garrison_factor_bulgarian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_required_garrison_factor_bulgarian
			min = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_bulgarian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_bulgarian
			max = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_resistance = yes
				is_core_of = ROOT
				TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
			}
			cancel_resistance = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = BUL }
			remove_core_of = BUL
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = MAC }
			remove_core_of = MAC
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_reduce_bulgarian_population
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_bulgarian_minority
		custom_effect_tooltip = weekly_manpower_250
		set_variable = { TUR_ottoman_weekly_manpower_bulgarian = 0 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_resistance_decay_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_decay_bulgarian = 0.1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_resistance_decay_bulgarian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_required_garrison_factor_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_required_garrison_factor_bulgarian = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_required_garrison_factor_bulgarian
			min = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_recruitable_population_bulgarian_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_bulgarian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_bulgarian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
	}
	add_to_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_state_level = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_level = -20 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_orthodox_state_level = -1 }
}

TUR_reduce_albanian_christian_level = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { TUR_albanian_state_level < 2 } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { TUR_ottoman_army_core_defence_factor_albanian > 0 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_army_core_defence_factor_albanian_down
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_army_core_defence_factor_albanian = -0.01 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_growth_albanian = -0.1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_resistance_growth_albanian
			min = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_albanian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_albanian
			max = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_resistance = yes
				is_core_of = ROOT
				TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
			}
			cancel_resistance = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = ALB }
			remove_core_of = ALB
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_reduce_albanian_population
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_albanian_minority
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { TUR_ottoman_army_core_defence_factor_albanian > 0 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_army_core_defence_factor_albanian_down
			add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_army_core_defence_factor_albanian = -0.01 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_growth_albanian = -0.1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_resistance_growth_albanian
			min = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_recruitable_population_albanian_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_albanian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_albanian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
	}
	add_to_variable = { TUR_albanian_state_level = -1 }
	hidden_effect = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_level }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_level = 0 }
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_level = -20 }
				add_to_variable = { TUR_orthodox_state_level = -1 }
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = TUR_albanian_catholic_state_level }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_catholic_state_level = 0 }
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { TUR_albanian_catholic_state_level = -20 }
				add_to_variable = { TUR_catholic_state_level = -1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

				ROOT = { country_event = { id = ottoman.139 days = 4 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_resistance = yes
				is_core_of = ROOT
				TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = no
			}
			cancel_resistance = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_core_of = ARM }
			remove_core_of = ARM
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_reduce_assyrian_population
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_assyrian_minority
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_mehmed_resid_committee } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = monthly_population_100
		}
		else = { custom_effect_tooltip = weekly_manpower_250 }
		set_variable = { TUR_ottoman_weekly_manpower_assyrian = 0 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_local_org_regain_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_local_org_regain_assyrian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_local_org_regain_assyrian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_minority_recruitable_population_assyrian_down
		add_to_variable = { TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_assyrian = 0.05 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = TUR_ottoman_recruitable_population_factor_assyrian
			max = 0
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
	}
	add_to_variable = { TUR_assyrian_state_level = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_assyrian_syriac_state_level = -20 }
	add_to_variable = { TUR_syriac_state_level = -1 }
}

					set_variable = { TUR_vc_required_garrison_factor = -100 }
					set_temp_variable = { TUR_vc_required_garrison_factor_temp = -1.15 }

TUR_minority_system_kosova = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = 0 }
		if = { #KOSOVA
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_kosova
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #KOSOVA SUNNI ALBANIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_kosova
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_kosova
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova = TUR_albanian_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova = TUR_albanian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova }
			if = { #KOSOVA ORTHODOX SERBS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_kosova
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_serbian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_serbian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_kosova
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_serbian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_serbian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = TUR_serbian_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = TUR_serbian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova }
			if = { #KOSOVA ORTHODOX BULGARIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_kosova
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_kosova
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = TUR_bulgarian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova }
			if = { #KOSOVA SUNNI TURKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_kosova
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_kosova
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kosova = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kosova = ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kosova = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kosova = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kosova = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_kosova_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_kosova = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kosova }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_kosova = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_kosova_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_kosova }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kosova = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kosova = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_kosova }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kosova = ROOT.TUR_serbian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kosova = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_kosova }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kosova = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kosova }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kosova = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kosova = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kosova }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kosova }
	}
}

						has_state_flag = TUR_iskodra
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_catholic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_iskodra
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_catholic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra = TUR_albanian_catholic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra = TUR_albanian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra = TUR_catholic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_iskodra = 0 }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_iskodra = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_iskodra = ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_iskodra = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_iskodra }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_iskodra_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_iskodra = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_iskodra }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_iskodra = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_iskodra_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_iskodra }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_minority_value_iskodra = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_iskodra }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_minority_value_iskodra = ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_iskodra = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_iskodra = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra = 2 }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_iskodra = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_iskodra = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_iskodra }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_iskodra = ROOT.TUR_albanian_catholic_minority_value_iskodra }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_iskodra = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_iskodra = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_iskodra }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_iskodra }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_manastir = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #MANASTIR
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #MANASTIR ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_greek_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
			if = { #MANASTIR ORTHODOX BULGARIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_bulgarian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
			if = { #MANASTIR SUNNI ALBANIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir = TUR_albanian_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir = TUR_albanian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
			if = { #MANASTIR ORTHODOX ALBANIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_albanian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
			if = { #MANASTIR SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_manastir_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_manastir = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_manastir_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_manastir }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir }
	}
}

							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya = TUR_albanian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = 0 }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_yanya }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_yanya }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_yanya_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_yanya = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_yanya_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_yanya }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_minority_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_iskodra }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_minority_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_iskodra }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_yanya = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_yanya = 2 }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_yanya }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_yanya }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_selanik = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #SELANIK
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #SELANIK ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_greek_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
			if = { #SELANIK ORTHODOX BULGARIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_bulgarian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
			if = { #SELANIK JUDAIC JEWS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_jewish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik

					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = TUR_jewish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = TUR_judaic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik }
			if = { #SELANIK SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_selanik_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_selanik = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_selanik_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_selanik }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik }
	}
}

				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_edirne
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_edirne }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_edirne_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_edirne = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_edirne_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_edirne }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_edirne = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_edirne = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_edirne = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_edirne = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne }
	}
}

	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #CATALCA

					}

							}

					}
				}

				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_izmit = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_izmit = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_izmit = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_izmit }
			if = { #IZMIT SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_izmit
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_izmit = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_izmit
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_izmit = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_izmit = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_izmit = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_izmit = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_izmit = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_izmit = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_izmit }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_izmit = 0 }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_izmit = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_izmit }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_izmit = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_izmit }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_izmit = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_izmit }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_izmit = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_izmit = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_izmit }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_izmit }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_archipelago = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #ARCHIPELAGO
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_archipelago
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #ARCHIPELAGO ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_archipelago
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_archipelago
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = TUR_greek_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago }
			if = { #ARCHIPELAGO SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_archipelago
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_archipelago
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_archipelago_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_archipelago = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_archipelago_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_archipelago }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago }
	}
}

				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_biga
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_biga = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_biga = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_biga = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_biga = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_biga = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_biga = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_biga }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_biga = 0 }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_biga = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_biga }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_biga = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_biga }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_biga = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_biga = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_biga }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_biga }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_hudavendigar = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #HUDAVENDIGAR
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_hudavendigar
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #HUDAVENDIGAR APOSTOLIC ARMENIAN
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_hudavendigar
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
				every_state = {

					if = {

							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_armenian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar }
			if = { #HUDAVENDIGAR ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_hudavendigar
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_hudavendigar
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }

					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_greek_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar }
			if = { #HUDAVENDIGAR SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_hudavendigar
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_hudavendigar
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_hudavendigar_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_hudavendigar = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_hudavendigar_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_hudavendigar }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar }
	}
}
