					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aydin = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aydin }
	}

				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kastamonu = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_kastamonu = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kastamonu = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kastamonu = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_kastamonu }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kastamonu = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_kastamonu }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kastamonu = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kastamonu = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_kastamonu }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_kastamonu }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_ankara = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #ANKARA
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_ankara
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #ANKARA APOSTOLIC ARMENIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_ankara
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_ankara
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara = TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara = TUR_armenian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara }
			if = { #ANKARA ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_ankara
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_ankara
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara = TUR_greek_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara }
			if = { #ANKARA SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_ankara
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_ankara
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_ankara_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_ankara = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_ankara_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_ankara }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara }
	}
}

				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_adana_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_adana = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_adana }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_adana = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_adana_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_adana }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_adana = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_adana = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_adana = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_adana = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_adana = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_adana }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_adana = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_adana }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_adana = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_adana }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_adana = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_adana = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_adana }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_adana }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_sivas = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #SIVAS
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #SIVAS APOSTOLIC ARMENIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas = TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas = TUR_armenian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas }
			if = { #SIVAS ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas = TUR_greek_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas }
			if = { #SIVAS SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas }
			if = { #SIVAS SHIA TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_shia_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_shia_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = TUR_turkish_shia_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = TUR_shia_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_sivas_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_sivas = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_sivas_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_sivas }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas }
	}
}

		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_trabzon = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_trabzon = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_trabzon }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_trabzon }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_erzurum = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #ERZURUM
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_erzurum
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #ERZURUM APOSTOLIC ARMENIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_erzurum
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_erzurum
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_armenian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum }
			if = { #ERZURUM SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_erzurum
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_erzurum
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
			if = { #ERZURUM SUNNI KURDISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_erzurum
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_erzurum
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_kurdish_population_factor_new }

			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
			if = { #ERZURUM orthodox georgian
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_erzurum
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_georgian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_georgian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_erzurum
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_georgian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_georgian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_georgian_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_georgian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_erzurum_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_erzurum = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_erzurum_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_erzurum }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum }
	}
}

				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_diyarbakir = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #DIYARBAKIR
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_diyarbakir
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #DIYARBAKIR APOSTOLIC ARMENIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_diyarbakir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_diyarbakir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_armenian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir }
			if = { #DIYARBAKIR SYRIAC ASSYRIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_diyarbakir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_assyrian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_assyrian_syriac_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_diyarbakir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_assyrian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_assyrian_syriac_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_assyrian_syriac_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_assyrian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_syriac_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir }
			if = { #DIYARBAKIR SUNNI KURDISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_diyarbakir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_diyarbakir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_kurdish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir }
			if = { #DIYARBAKIR SUNNI ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_diyarbakir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_diyarbakir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_arab_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_diyarbakir_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_diyarbakir = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_diyarbakir_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_diyarbakir }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir }
	}
}

					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }

					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = TUR_armenian_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis }
			if = { #BITLIS SUNNI KURDISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_bitlis
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_bitlis
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = TUR_sunni_kurdish_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_bitlis_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_bitlis = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_bitlis_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_bitlis }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis }
	}
}

						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_van
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_van
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = TUR_kurdish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_van_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_van = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_van = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_van_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_van }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van }
	}
}

								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}

				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }

					}

				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_aleppo
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }

					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_kurdish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }

			if = { #ALEPPO SUNNI TURKISH

						has_state_flag = TUR_aleppo

					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aleppo }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_aleppo_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_aleppo = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_aleppo_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_aleppo }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo }
	}
}

						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul = TUR_arab_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
			if = { #MOSUL SUNNI KURDISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_mosul
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_mosul
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = TUR_kurdish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
			if = { #MOSUL SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_mosul
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_mosul
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = TUR_turkish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mosul = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_mosul }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_mosul_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mosul }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_mosul = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_mosul_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_mosul }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mosul = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_mosul }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mosul = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mosul }
	}
}

						check_variable = { TUR_arab_catholic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut = TUR_arab_catholic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut = TUR_arab_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut = TUR_catholic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut }
			if = { #BEIRUT JUDAIC JEWISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_beirut
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_jewish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_beirut
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_jewish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut = TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut = TUR_jewish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut = TUR_judaic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_beirut }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = 0 }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_beirut }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_beirut_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_beirut = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_beirut_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_beirut }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_jerusalem = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #JERUSALEM
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_jerusalem
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #JERUSALEM SUNNI ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_jerusalem
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_jerusalem = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_jerusalem
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_jerusalem = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_jerusalem = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_jerusalem = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_jerusalem = TUR_arab_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_jerusalem = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_jerusalem = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_jerusalem }
			if = { #JERUSALEM JUDAIC JEWISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_jerusalem
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_jewish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_jerusalem = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_jerusalem
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_jewish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_jerusalem = TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_jerusalem = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_jerusalem = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_jerusalem = TUR_jewish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_jerusalem = TUR_judaic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_jerusalem = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_jerusalem }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_jerusalem = 0 }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_jerusalem = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_jerusalem }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_jerusalem = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_jerusalem }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_jerusalem = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_jerusalem = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_jerusalem }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_jerusalem }
	}

		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_basra }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_basra }

TUR_minority_system_hejaz = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #HEJAZ
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_hejaz
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #HEJAZ SUNNI ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_hejaz
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_hejaz
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz = TUR_arab_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hejaz = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hejaz = -0.005 }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz = 2 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hejaz = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_hejaz }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hejaz = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hejaz = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hejaz }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hejaz }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_yemen = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #YEMEN
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_yemen
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #YEMEN SUNNI ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_yemen
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_yemen
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen = TUR_arab_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen }
			if = { #YEMEN SHIA ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_yemen
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_shia_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_yemen
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_shia_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen = TUR_arab_shia_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
					}
				}
				ultiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen = TUR_arab_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen = TUR_shia_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen }
			if = { #YEMEN JUDAIC JEWISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_yemen
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_jewish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_yemen
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_jewish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = TUR_jewish_population_factor_new }
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = TUR_judaic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_yemen_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_yemen = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_yemen_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_yemen }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen }
	}
}

			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_alexandria = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_alexandria = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_alexandria }
	}
